# T-116 capture index (pass 1: capture only)

Audit task: [`docs/work/T-116-audit-ui-ux.md`](../../../docs/work/T-116-audit-ui-ux.md). Commit examined: `569ae1f` (branch `t-116`). Captured on 2026-10-12 by an automated Playwright driver (Chromium, headless) against a test-mode dev server (`vite --mode test`, port 5311). This index records what was done and what the game showed. It contains no judgement, severity or recommendation.

## Conventions

- Window 1280x800 unless a step row says otherwise. At zoom 1 that is a grid of 80 columns by 50 rows (cells 16 px). The fortress screen puts the map on rows 0-44, the log strip on rows 45-47 (three announcement lines) and the two status rows on rows 48-49. An open panel takes the right 36 columns.
- Every step has a screenshot (`.png`) and a cell-buffer dump (`.txt`). The dump header lists the exact keys and mouse actions of the step, the tick, whether the clock was paused (speed 0), the screen id, the open panel, the viewport and grid size, and console errors/warnings since the previous step. The grid follows as `NN|text` lines, then the log strip rows and the status rows. Cell colours are only in the screenshot.
- "Keys / clicks" are verbatim: `key X` is a real keyboard press through the browser; `left-click cell(col C, row R) px(X,Y)` is a real mouse click at the centre of that cell; `hook step(n)` / `hook step to tick n` is a call to the test hook's `step`, used only to advance the clock (the clock is otherwise paused, as the fortress opens paused); `hook submit` is a call to the hook's command call.
- `[KEY - reason]` after a key marks a point where a mouse-only run had to use the keyboard because no mouse way was found; the reason is what was tried.
- "What the game showed" is taken from the buffer: for the fortress screen the three log-strip lines, the two status rows and (with a panel open) the first lines of the panel; for other screens the first non-blank lines of the screen (for the world map, the right-hand panel).
- "screen unchanged from step N; no screenshot saved" means the cell buffer (glyphs and colours) was identical to the previous step. The dump file is still saved.
- Tick numbers after a `Space` resume advance with real time while the clock runs (a few ticks per second at 1x).
- Default random seeds are repeatable in this capture because `Math.random` is replaced by a fixed generator before the page loads (the game builds its default seed `deep-<n>-<k>` from `Math.random`). This changes nothing else in the game.
- Console: warnings and errors (`console.error`, `console.warn`), uncaught exceptions (`pageerror`) and failed requests were collected for every step.


## Flow 1: New player, mouse only, from the main menu

Method: Playwright mouse events only (click, right-click, drag, wheel, middle drag), starting at the main menu with the default seed, default party and default supplies. World: standard size, region "Bitterbrae" (tundra, wealth 2, peril 0, temper 3), reached by clicking a land cell. Where the mouse had no way to do the next thing, the step records the attempt and the next step uses one key press, marked `[KEY - ...]`; from there the run is mouse plus those keys. Map positions for placements were chosen with the hook's readers (setup only, not an input): free 3x3 patches near the wagon.

Mouse could do (observed): every main-menu row; "[g] Generate" and "[Enter] Continue" on the world screen; choose a world-map cell; region panel buttons `[f] filter`, `[f] close`, `[?] legend`, `[?] close`, `[Enter] Embark here`; embark tabs, a dwarf row, `[p]`, `[Enter] Embark`; click a tile (Look), a dwarf, a building (building panel), the date (Calendar panel); right click (Look, or cancel a tool); wheel (z level); middle drag (pan); Shift+wheel; Ctrl+wheel down (zoom out); rows and buttons inside an open panel (Dig, Chop tree, Stairwell, Stockpile, Farm plot, Meeting area, Bedroom, category rows, workshop and furniture rows, `[S-n] new order`, a recipe row, `[Enter] add`, the stockpile preset header and options, `[+]` buttons, crop dropdowns); drag a rectangle for a designation, a zone and a building; the game menu's "Rename fortress" row.

Mouse could not do (observed, each tried): open any fortress panel other than Calendar (by date), Look (by tile) and Building (by building) - the hotkey strip in status row 2, `[?] help`, the population, mood, weather, z-level and speed fields were clicked and nothing opened; Designate, Build, Zones, Orders, Units, Jobs, Labors, Stocks, Military, Animals, Threats, Civilizations, Population, Mood, Log, Overlays, Help and Debug were opened by key; open the game menu (Esc); pause, resume or change speed (the speed glyph was clicked, no change; Space was used); close a panel (the `[d]` in the title row was clicked, no change; the sidebar stayed until Esc was pressed); scroll the order picker's recipe list (wheel over the sidebar changed the z level instead; a click on the scrollbar track and a drag of its thumb changed nothing; only recipes in the visible part were reachable); type a filter or a quantity; set a priority 1-9 (the "Priority: 5" row was clicked, no change); `[Ctrl+Z] undo last` and `[Del] clear` rows clicked, no visible change; Ctrl+wheel up at zoom 1 (no visible change).

Steps: 122. Files: `capture/1-mouse-only/<nn>-<slug>.png` and `.txt`.

## Flow 2: New player, keyboard only, from the main menu

Method: Playwright keyboard events only (the mouse was never moved or clicked), from the main menu, same default seed (`deep-3588-1`), same region as flow 1 (region id 2339, "Bitterbrae"), default party and supplies. The `Enter` placements aim by panning the camera until the tile wanted is the map-centre tile (a keyboard user has no other way to aim, see below); the number of key presses used to aim is in each step's text. Free 3x3 patches near the wagon were found with the hook's readers (setup only). The only non-key action besides the hook's `step` is none.

Keyboard could do (observed): the whole menu, world generation, walking the world-map cursor to a region (7 key presses: `Shift+ArrowLeft`/`ArrowLeft x6`/`ArrowUp x4` in the dump), embark (`Enter` on the embark screen embarked at once), every panel by letter, designations, stockpile, farm plot, meeting area, bedroom, workshop, door tool, an order by typing a filter, the stockpile preset dropdown, game menu, rename.

Keyboard could not do (observed, each tried):
- Choose a crop for a farm plot. In the farm plot editor these keys were tried and the four season dropdowns stayed "none": `ArrowDown` (pans the map), `Enter`, `ArrowDown`, `Enter`, `1`, `ArrowRight`, `Space` (resumes the clock), `Space` (pauses it again).
- Open the Calendar panel: `Tab` (toggles the sidebar), `Tab`, `F2`, `F3` were tried; it opens by clicking the date.
- Aim at a tile other than the map-centre tile (or a look cursor set by `k`): `Enter` with a tool uses the look cursor if one is set, else the map centre. With the sidebar open the camera cannot go past x = 0, so the left-most tile that can be the map centre is x = 22 (probe step `probe-left-edge`: after `Shift+ArrowLeft x5` the centre was x=22, camera x=0); the wagon is at x=1, y=47. Tiles with x < 22 (with a panel open) were not reachable by `Enter`.
- Hover for the tile summary or tooltips, double-click to centre on a unit, drag-select a rectangle for item commands: no key was found for these in this run (not individually tried).
Quirk observed: pressing `k` twice closes the Look panel but leaves the look cursor set (dump header `look-cursor`); later `Enter` presses with a tool then acted at the cursor tile, not at the map centre. `Escape` with a panel open clears it. This was found in an earlier run of this script; the final run clears the cursor with `k`, `Escape` before the designations.

## Flow 3: A mature fortress (everyday tasks, with key and click counts)

Fortresses used. The harness offers `test/fixtures/reference-fortress.deepholm` (the 80-dwarf reference fortress) and `test/fixtures/played-fortress.deepholm` (20 dwarves, played 4,200 ticks by a script). Both were loaded through the real screens: main menu `l` (Load), then the import key `i`, then `Enter` on the imported save. The `i` press had to go through the hook (`h.key('i')`), because a real key press did not raise the browser's file chooser in this harness (the existing `export-import` spec also uses the hook for it). The caravan for the trade task was brought with the hook, as the task allows ("only for state").

What the reference fortress contains (read through the hook's readers): 80 dwarves; 99 beds, 100 chairs, 100 tables, 100 statues, 100 chain posts, 100 paved floors, one wagon; 15 stockpile zones (4 tiles each, on rows y=3..9 of level z27) and 15 bedroom zones; no workshop, no trade depot, no farm plot, no squad. It opens paused on Day 1 of Spring, Year 3 (tick 269201), world hash `2d9920c34c319d0e`. After stepping 1,200 ticks (hook, setup) the log shows "A raid has arrived! x2", "Hrolf Brighteye has lost consciousness." and later "The raid has ended.", the status row shows `+1` patient and `!4` hostiles, the Threats panel lists "Active: bandits (4, engage)", and a Hillfolk caravan party is in state `waiting` although there is no depot. In the labors grid every labor of every dwarf visible was on (all `■`). The played fortress has carpenter, craftsdwarf, kitchen, mason, mechanic and still workshops, one squad ("Gate", 4 members), one farm plot, two stockpiles, no forge, no depot.

Setup done by the hook, not by the UI (and so not counted): `SetLabor mining off` for the first dwarf (task 1, so that there is a labor to give); stepping 1,200 ticks (reference fortress) or 600 ticks (played fortress); for the trade task a finished trade depot, a request, 20 easy meals moved into the depot, the first dwarf given the broker title, stepping until the caravan was at the depot, and stepping tick by tick until the trade session opened.

Counts (keys = real key presses; clicks = real mouse clicks; the setup above and the `hook step` calls are not counted; typed characters are counted separately). Raw numbers are in [`capture/3-mature-fortress/counts.json`](capture/3-mature-fortress/counts.json).

| Task (as done) | Keys | Typed characters | Clicks | Steps and result |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1. Find an idle dwarf and give it a labor (keyboard): `u`, `u`, `l`, `Enter` (labor manager), `ArrowRight` x19, `Enter`, `Escape` | 25 | 0 | 0 | The first `u` after a pinned danger line only dismissed the line (step `t1-u-first`: no panel); the idle dwarf (row 0 of the Units list, "No job") needed 0 `ArrowDown`; `Enter` in the Labors panel opened the labor manager, whose header read "Vera Deepmark, Miner · Medicine: off · priority 5" in an earlier trial where `ArrowRight` x18 was used; with x19 the hook's reader reports mining enabled for the first dwarf afterwards. Idle dwarves in the list: several rows read "No j..." (the Act. column shows 4 characters). |
| 2. Change a stockpile's contents, keyboard attempt: `z`, panning to the stockpile, `Enter` | 3 | 0 | 0 | Not possible in this fortress: the 15 stockpiles are on rows y=3..9 and the map-centre tile cannot be above y=22 (camera clamped); status row: "There is no zone at the cursor. Centre the map on a zone and press Enter." |
| 2. Change a stockpile's contents, mouse: `z`, click the stockpile tile, click the "Preset [" header, click "Drink" | 1 | 0 | 3 | The editor opened ("Selected: Stockpile #2 (4 t)", "Preset [Custom]"), the preset header opened a list (Ore and bars, Wood, Furniture, Weapons and armor, Cloth and leather ...); after the last click the dump shows the category list with `[ ]` boxes (see step `t2-m-drink`). |
| 3. Find a wounded dwarf and see its treatment: `u`, `ArrowDown` x20 (to the dwarf named in the log), `Enter` (Look), `3` (Creatures tab), `m` | 24 | 0 | 0 | The Look panel's Creature tab shows "Mood: Unhappy (36.686247314646366)" with a list of thoughts and the line "Health: Wounded, bleeding". No line naming a hospital, a physician or a treatment is shown there. The status row `+1` is not clickable (flow 1 probe). |
| 4. Raise the alert, make a squad of four, set it Active, arm a kill order, look at the gear keys: `t`, `m`, `3`, `+`, `Shift+A`, (`Space`,`ArrowDown`) x4, `Enter`, `v`, `Shift+K`, pan x?, `Enter`, `w`, `g`, `Shift+J` | 21 | 0 | 0 | Log: "The fortress alert is now hearth." After `Shift+K` the panel reads "Orders Click creature to attack". The target is picked by a mouse click; with the keyboard `Enter` at the map centre was tried on the bandit's tile (steps `t4-kill-aimed`, `t4-kill-enter`). The raid had already ended by this time ("! The raid has ended." in the log). |
| 5. Why is an order not being made, add and read (played fortress): `o`, `Shift+N`, type "mail" (4 characters), `Enter`, then read the Orders list | 3 | 4 | 0 | Status row: "Ordered: x1 Mail shirt · any forge". After 300 ticks the Orders panel lists the order under "Any workshop" as "x1 Mail shirt · no forge". |
| 5b. The same, looking in the Jobs panel: `j`, `Shift+S` x2 | 3 | 0 | 0 | The Jobs panel (steps `B-jobs`...) lists jobs (`stop bleeding`, `craft still`, ...); the order is not listed there. |
| 5c. An order that has a workshop (played fortress): `o`, `Shift+N`, type "barrel" (6), `Tab`, `Tab`, `ArrowRight`, type "5", `Enter` | 6 | 7 | 0 | Status row: "Ordered: ≥5 Metal barrel · any forge" (the first match for "barrel" is the metal barrel). |
| 6. Mark goods and trade with a caravan (fresh default embark, caravan brought by the hook): `Escape`, `c`, `Shift+D`, `Shift+T`, `ArrowDown`, `+`, `Tab`, `End`, `Enter`, `Escape` | 10 | 0 | 0 | The trade session opened as "Trading with the ..." (dump `C-trade-session`); an offer was made with `Enter`; the session closed with `Escape`. |

## Flow 4: The newer UI (order picker, labor manager, building panel, drag-to-place, stockpile editor, room panel, tooltips, confirmations, Shift accelerators, filters, animals, rename, notices)

Setup: embark at region 2339 ("Bitterbrae") of seed `deep-3588-1` by keyboard (setup keys are not in the step table; the embark-screen steps are), default party and supplies, fortress opens paused at tick 400. Free 3x3 patches were found with the hook's readers (setup only). The smallest window used is 960x540: at the smallest zoom (0.75, cells of 12 px) it gives the minimum grid of 80x45 cells (`MIN_VIEW_COLS` x `MIN_VIEW_ROWS` in `src/core/constants.ts`); the sidebar stays 36 columns wide at both sizes (the dump header of each step gives the grid). Panels were opened at 1280x800 (steps `B-<panel>-1280x800`) and at 960x540 (`B-<panel>-960x540`), including Designate, Build, Zones, Units, Jobs, Labors, Orders, Stocks, Military, Animals (the two-column panels), Threats, Civilizations, Population, Mood, Log, Overlays and Help.

The hook was used only for setup: `completeNow` on the carpenter's workshop and the beds (step `E-completed`), and `hook step` to let a new zone be seen by the zone system (one tick). The embark questions are steps `A-embark-no-axe` ("Embark without an axe?" with `[y] Yes  [n] No`) and `A-embark-no-pick`. Where a step presses `Escape` to answer a confirmation, the next step shows what the game did. Several `Escape` presses inside the stockpile editor's sub-focuses (tree, numeric settings) were followed by the panel being closed (panel `null` in the dump header); the script then re-selected the stockpile before continuing (the step text says so where it happened).
After the window was resized to 960x540 and back to 1280x800 (step `B-back-1280`), the grid stayed 106x66 (cells of 12 px) for the rest of this flow; the dump headers of steps 48 onward show 106x66.

Steps: 132. Files: `capture/4-newer-ui/<nn>-<slug>.png` and `.txt`.

## Flow 5: Failure paths

Setup: embark at region 2339 of seed `deep-3588-1` by keyboard; fortress opens paused at tick 400. Each step records what the game showed in the status row, log strip or dialog (verbatim in the table). Not captured, with what was tried:
- "Assign a labor to a child": neither fixture nor the default embark has a child dwarf (the hook reports 0 children in the reference fortress; a default embark has none). No step.
- "A stockpile with no room": no step in this flow. The log line "Stockpile #2 (Everything) is full." appears in flow 3 (played fortress, steps `B-orders-after-300`).
- "A save name that is too long": pressing Enter on "Save" in the game menu did not open a name input (the menu stayed; the Load list was opened afterwards, steps `S2`..`S3`). The only named-text inputs reachable from the menu were rename fortress, dwarf, squad; the 200-character test was done on "Rename fortress" (steps `S4`..`S6`). In the game menu the letter keys are accelerators, so an earlier trial that typed 200 `x` characters into the menu itself triggered the Retire dialog (not kept in this run).
- Trade refusals: steps `T1`..`T7` (part B of this flow; fresh embark with a caravan brought by the hook for state).
- The embark with nothing: steps `E1`..`E8` (every supply and animal count reduced by key presses; the questions that followed are in the steps).

Steps: 101. Files: `capture/5-failure-paths/<nn>-<slug>.png` and `.txt`.

## Flow 6: Legibility (palettes, window sizes, long names, full log, help, legend)

Setup: session 1 starts at the main menu with defaults, toggles the colorblind palette in Settings (steps `A-*`), embarks at region 2339 of seed `deep-3588-1` with the colorblind palette on, makes a stockpile, a farm plot, a bedroom and a chop designation so that zone and designation tints are on screen (`B-*`), toggles the palette back (`C-*`, same site), then window sizes (`E-*`), long names (`F-*`), Help pages (`G-*`) and the Zoom setting (`D-*`, last, because it changes the grid for the rest of the session). Session 2 is the 80-dwarf reference fortress stepped 7,200 ticks, to have many log lines (`H-*`). Session 3 retires a fortress to reach the legend screen (`I-*`). Window sizes tried, with the grid each gave (see the dump headers): 1280x800 -> 80x50, 1920x1080 -> 120x67, 2560x1440 -> 160x90, 3840x2160 -> 240x135, 960x540 -> 80x45, 640x360 -> 80x45 (below 960x540 the 80x45 grid is kept and the canvas is scaled). The smallest size at which the minimum grid fits without scaling (960x540) is the smallest used in flow 4. Observed in flow 4 (step `B-back-1280` onward) and again at the end of this flow (`E-back-1280x800-fortress`): after the window is made smaller than the zoom allows and then returned to 1280x800, the grid stays at the smaller cell size (106x66 cells instead of 80x50).

Long names: the dwarf rename input (units panel, `Shift+N`) was given 30 characters, then 26 (the spec limit is 24); the fortress rename was given 39 characters (the limit is 32); steps `F-*` show what each panel then displayed. The Help panel was paged with `PageDown` (steps `G-help-1` .. `G-help-8`). The Log panel was paged in the reference fortress (steps `H-log-*`); its filter keys are in steps `H-log-tab` and `H-log-slash`. The colour palettes are only visible in the screenshots (the dumps hold glyphs, not colours).

Steps: 88. Files: `capture/6-legibility/<nn>-<slug>.png` and `.txt`.
